Let's formulate objective carefully for LP and gradients.

Given h length n, dx=2/n.
Define directed lags:
For k=0: f0 = ∑ h_i(1-h_i). Gradient f0_i = 1 - 2h_i.
For 1≤k≤n-1:
P_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_i (1 - h_{i+k}) (positive shift: h at lower index times complement higher)
Q_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} h_{i+k} (1 - h_i) (negative shift? h higher times complement lower)
Objective F = max( max P_k, max Q_k, P0 ) *dx.
Compute gradients:
P0 = ∑ h_i(1-h_i): grad 1-2h_i.
For P_k:
For i<n-k: derivative wrt h_i: (1 - h_{i+k}); for i≥k: derivative wrt h_i from being higher with base i-k: - h_{i-k}.
Thus gradP[i] = (i<n-k ? 1-h[i+k] : 0) - (i>=k ? h[i-k] : 0).
For Q_k:
Q_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} h_{i+k}(1-h_i).
For h_j where j is higher (j=i+k, j=k..n-1): derivative (1-h_{j-k}). For h_j where j is lower (j=i, j=0..n-k-1): derivative -h_{j+k}.
Thus gradQ[i] = (i>=k ? 1-h[i-k] : 0) - (i<n-k ? h[i+k] : 0).
Check sum gradient? For any constant shift δ, derivative of P_k = ∑ grad_i δ = δ [(∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k}(1-h_{i+k})) - ∑_{i=k}^{n-1} h_i] = δ[(n-k) - sum_high - sum_high] not zero; but feasible sum fixed and bounds.

Linearization row for P_k active: L=P_k; g=gradP. For Q similarly. If both same max, include both.

Incremental algorithm details:
- h list of 0/1 ints length n, count ones = n/2.
- P, Q lists length n (0 unused) compute initial:
for i in range(n):
  hi=h[i]
  for j in range(i+1,n):
    if hi==1 and h[j]==0: P[j-i]+=1
    elif hi==0 and h[j]==1: Q[j-i]+=1
O(n^2) per init.
- For swap a (old 1 ->0) and b (old 0 ->1).
Steps:
  # update all pairs involving a with r != b
  e=a; old=1; new=0
  for r in range(n): if r==b: continue; d=abs(e-r); left=e if e<r else r; right=e if e<r else r? Need determine left bit and right bit before swap.
    if r < e:
       # left=r old_h[r]; right=e old bit
       L=old_h[r]; R=old
       # old contrib: if L=1,R=0 P else if L=0,R=1 Q
       # new contrib: R=new
    else: # r > e
       L=old; R=h[r]
    compute oldP, oldQ, newP,newQ and update P[d],Q[d]
  # update all pairs involving b with r != a (b old 0 ->1)
  e=b; old=0; new=1 similar.
  # update pair (a,b): sort left/right old bits 1 and0; new bits 0 and1
    d=abs(a-b); if a<b: old L=h[a]=1,R=h[b]=0 => old P[d] 1, Q0; new L=0,R=1 => new Q1. So P[d]-=1; Q[d]+=1. If a>b: old L=h[b]=0,R=h[a]=1 => old Q1; new P1. So Q[d]-=1; P[d]+=1.
  # Now h[a]=0; h[b]=1.
Important: In endpoint updates excluding other endpoint, using old_h array (unchanged) for other bits. For b endpoint old=0/new=1, pair with r (not a). Good.
After updates, new max = max(max(P), max(Q)) (exclude P0/Q0? P[0]=0 always). Could speed by tracking current max and only recompute if updates changed values near max? Simpler recompute built-in max lists length n=100; fast.
- Accepting move: new_max < cur_max or (==cur_max and maybe diversity) or anneal. If accept, keep h,P,Q. If not, revert: We need either store old P/Q/h? Since we updated in place, for reject can reapply reverse swap (swap same a,b) restoring P/Q,h. We can implement a `perform_swap(h,P,Q,a,b)` that mutates to swapped state and returns new_max. If reject, call perform_swap again with a,b? But after swap, a is 0, b is1; swapping back will work (a old 0->1, b old1->0). However our function assumes a=1,b=0. We can generalize `swap_bits` that flips both bits and updates based on old bits. Then reject by swapping back. Need initial current max? We can compute after. To avoid residual errors, maybe full recompute occasionally.

But function using old bits: for each e oldbit=h[e], newbit=1-oldbit. For swap two positions, set new bits. We can call with a,b general; for reject, call again (bits swapped) to revert. Need update pair (a,b) correctly based on old bits before function.

Pseudo:
def swap_bits(h,P,Q,a,b):
    # assumes h[a]!=h[b]
    d_ab=abs(a-b)
    old_a=h[a]; old_b=h[b]
    # update endpoint a with r != b using old bits
    e=a; old=old_a; new=1-old
    for r in range(n): if r == b: continue
       if r < e:
          L=h[r]; R=old
       else:
          L=old; R=h[r]
       oldP = 1 if L==1 and R==0 else 0
       ...
       deltaP=newP-oldP; if delta: P[d]+=delta
    # endpoint b similarly r != a; old_b, new_b
    # pair a,b: old contribution from old_a/old_b; new from new_a/new_b. Since they are opposite, contribution before/after between P,Q. Compute generic:
       if a < b:
          old_L=old_a; old_R=old_b; new_L=new_a; new_R=new_b
       else: swap
       oldP ... newP; P[d_ab]+=newP-oldP, Q...
    h[a]=new_a; h[b]=new_b
    return max(max(P), max(Q))

Could combine endpoints and pair by treating all other r? Simpler keep pair generic after endpoints excluding each other.

Need ensure if other endpoint's updates use old bits for r? In endpoint a loop, for r=b excluded; for r not b h still old bits. For endpoint b loop, h not changed yet, h[a] old_a. Good. Then pair update. Then set h.

Could optimize by precomputing arrays of P/Q? Use local variables for lists, n. Use `if deltaP:` branch. But computing oldP/newP with ifs per r might be okay. We can reduce computations due bits small: For L,R old/new, since only one bit changes, delta possible only if other bit leads. For endpoint e old->new, for each r we can derive delta without old/new four conditions:
If e is left (r>e): pair (e,r), L changes, R fixed b_r.
- P = L*(1-R). Old L=o, new=1-o. Delta P = R*(newL-oldL)? Because (1-R) constant, delta = (newL-oldL)*(1-R). If R=0 and L flips, delta = ±1; if R=1, 0.
- Q = (1-L)*R. Delta = (newQ-oldQ) = R*(oldL-newL) if? More simply if R=1, Q flips opposite: deltaQ = oldL - newL times R? Since (1-L)*R change = (oldL-newL)*R.
So if R bit known: deltaP=(new-old)*(1-R); deltaQ=(old-new)*R.
If e is right (r<e): pair (r,e), R changes, L fixed b_r.

Define compute_c5 exact for any h:
```
def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```
For binary int, `1.0-h` float; exact int maybe convert? If h int and 1-h int, use int. But for general float. For binary search, define compute_max_count_binary:
```
def binary_max_count(h):
    n=len(h); c=np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full'); return int(np.max(c))
```

Binary incremental:

Let's write `update_swap(h, P, Q, a, b)` with h list, P/Q lists, n.
Pseudo code optimized:
```
def binary_swap(h, P, Q, a, b):
    n = len(h)
    oa = h[a]; ob = h[b]
    na = 1 - oa; nb = 1 - ob
    sign_a = na - oa  # +1 if 0->1 else -1
    sign_b = nb - ob
    # endpoint a
    e = a
    st = sign_e
    # loop r in range(e)
    for r in range(e):
        L = h[r]  # left fixed, R changes
        # deltaP = -st * L; deltaQ = st * (1-L)
        if L:
            P[e-r] -= st
            # deltaQ=0 if L=1? st*(1-L)=0
        else:
            Q[e-r] += st
    for r in range(e+1,n):
        R = h[r]
        if R:
            Q[r-e] += -st  # deltaQ = -st*R
        else:
            P[r-e] += st
    # endpoint b similarly, but skip a. Since h not changed yet. Need loops skip if r==a.
    e = b; st=sign_b
    for r in range(e):
       if r == a: continue
       L=h[r]
       if L: P[e-r] -= st
       else: Q[e-r] += st
    for r in range(e+1,n):
       if r == a: continue
       R=h[r]
       if R: Q[r-e] -= st
       else: P[r-e] += st
    # pair a,b generic old/new
    d=abs(a-b)
    if a < b:
       oL=oa; oR=ob; nL=na; nR=nb
       oldP = 1 if oL and not oR else 0
       oldQ = 1 if not oL and oR else 0
       newP = 1 if nL and not nR else 0
       newQ = 1 if not nL and nR else 0
    else:
       oL=ob; oR=oa; nL=nb; nR=na
       ...
    P[d] += newP - oldP; Q[d] += newQ - oldQ
    h[a]=na; h[b]=nb
    # recompute max
    return max(max(P), max(Q))
```
Need verify endpoint formulas.
Case e left (r>e): pair (e,r), L=e changes, R fixed.
OldP=L_old*(1-R); newP=L_new*(1-R); deltaP=(L_new-L_old)*(1-R)=st*(1-R). If R=1 ->0; if R=0 -> +st. deltaQ=(1-L_new)*R - (1-L_old)*R = (L_old-L_new)*R = -st*R. If R=1 -> -st; R=0 0. Code: if R: Q[r-e]-=st else P[r-e]+=st. Good.
Case e right (r<e): pair (r,e), L fixed, R changes.
OldP=L*(1-R_old); newP=L*(1-R_new); deltaP=L*(R_old-R_new)= -st*L. If L=1 -> -st. deltaQ=(1-L)*R_new - (1-L)*R_old = (1-L)*st. If L=0 -> +st. Code: if L: P[e-r]-=st else Q[e-r]+=st. Good.
For endpoint b, h still old for a; skip a.

Pair old/new generic. Since oa,ob opposite, new opposite. But generic fine. Do we double count pair in endpoint loops if skip? yes skip.

After swap, max. For reject, call binary_swap with same a,b (now bits flipped). Check formulas with old bits new. Good.

But note if we call binary_swap to reject after accepted other moves? Only if immediate reject. In SA, if reject we revert immediately. Good. If accept, h/P/Q updated. No need old.

Potential max updates: P,Q can become negative due bug? Shouldn't. Could occasionally full recompute to correct.

Continuous objective and gradients efficient:
```
def directed_values(h):
    n=len(h)
    P = np.empty(n); Q=np.empty(n)
    P0 = np.sum(h*(1-h))
    P[0]=P0; Q[0]=P0
    for k in range(1,n):
       P[k] = np.dot(h[:-k], 1-h[k:])
       Q[k] = np.dot(h[k:], 1-h[:-k])
    return P,Q
```

Function `cont_dir_vals(h)`:
```
n=len(h); P=np.empty(n); Q=np.empty(n); P[0]=np.sum(h*(1-h)); Q[0]=P[0]
for k in range(1,n):
   seg_a=h[:-k]; seg_b=h[k:]
   P[k]=np.dot(seg_a, 1.0-seg_b)
   Q[k]=np.dot(seg_b, 1.0-seg_a)
return P,Q
```

def cont_grad_P(k,h):
 n=len; g=np.zeros(n)
 if k==0: g=1-2*h
 else:
   g[:n-k] += 1-h[k:]
   g[k:] -= h[:-k]
 return g
def cont_grad_Q(k,h):
 if k==0: ...
 else:
   g[k:] += 1-h[:-k]
   g[:n-k] -= h[k:]
```

LP step:
```
def cont_lp_step(h, P, Q, maxc, radius, tol=0.01):
    active indices list: for k in range(n): if P[k] > maxc - tol: add P; if Q[k] > maxc - tol: add Q. Include top maybe up to 50.
    Constraints: for active: grad dot d - t <= -val.
    variables d length n, t. c = [0]*n + [1].
    A_ub = rows nvars+1; b_ub = -vals.
    A_eq = ones_n + [0]; b_eq=0.
    bounds = [(lo,up)] for d + [(0,None)]
    lo[i]=max(-radius, -h[i]); up[i]=min(radius, 1-h[i])
    Use linprog c, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, method='highs'.
    if success: d=res.x[:n]; predicted=res.x[-1]
    return d, predicted
```
If active empty, use all. Limit active to e.g. 40 to keep fast: sort active by val, take those >= maxc - max(tol,0.05*maxc) maybe. Also include top 10. For n=100 rows 200 okay. linprog overhead maybe 0.01-0.05s. 100 iterations 5s.
Line search steps: `[1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.075,0.0375,0.01875]`. Candidate = project_sum(h + step*d). evaluate maxc. If best < current - 1e-12 accept. If no improve, maybe try negative d? Since maximizing? We minimize t; d should decrease. If predicted t < maxc but actual not, maybe step too large; if none, try subgradient random.

def binary_search_n(n, allowed, h_parent, seed, time_limit):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_m = None; best_seq=None
    for seq in starts:
       if elapsed > time_limit: break
       P,Q,m = init_binary_state(seq)
       # quick hill-climb/SA with time slice e.g. 20-60s
       m = binary_hill_sa(n, seq, P, Q, m, allowed, rng, time_slice)
       if best is None or m<best_m: update
       if m<=allowed: break maybe
    return best_seq,best_m
```

```
def random_composition(total, parts, max_len, rng):
    if parts<=0: return []
    lengths=[1]*parts
    rem=total-parts
    if rem<0: ...
    max_len=max(parts, max_len)
    while rem>0:
       # choose index with probability proportional to max_len - l + 1? or uniform
       idxs = np.nonzero(lengths < max_len)[0]
       if len(idxs)==0:
          idx=rng.randint(parts)
       else: idx=idxs[rng.randint(len(idxs))]
       lengths[idx]+=1; rem-=1
    return lengths
```
Uniform distribution can create max_len large after many, but with max_len 8 maybe after all saturated distribute remainder unconstrained. For total 50, parts19, max_len8, capacity 152, so okay. Distribution fairly uniform. Components mean 2.6. Good.

MILP formulation exact for binary and target B. We can specify variables: A_i binary (n), z_{i,j} binary for i<j (m=n(n-1)/2), B continuous. Objective minimize B (constant). Constraints:
1. sum A_i = n/2 (eq)
2. z_{i,j} ≤ A_i; z ≤ A_j; z ≥ A_i + A_j -1.
   This enforces z=A_i A_j for binary A. (3m constraints)
3. For each lag k=1..n-1:
   P_k = sum_{i=0}^{n-1-k} A_i - sum_{i=0}^{n-1-k} z_{i,i+k} ≤ B
   Q_k = sum_{i=k}^{n-1} A_i - sum_{i=0}^{n-1-k} z_{i,i+k} ≤ B
Constraints rows about (2(n-1) + 3m + 1). For n=100, m=4950, total ~15350 rows, 5051 vars. HiGHS can maybe handle. But milp may be slow. Could reduce constraints by using direct P/Q variables with linearization? Maybe use objective min max with constraints P/Q and z as above. Let's write update with d-loop:
```
def binary_swap(h, P, Q, a, b):
    n=len(h)
    oa=h[a]; ob=h[b]
    na=1-oa; nb=1-ob
    st=na-oa  # +-1
    e=a; other=b
    for d in range(1,n):
        idx=e-d
        if idx >=0 and idx != other:
            hr=h[idx]
            if hr: P[d] -= st
            else: Q[d] += st
        idx=e+d
        if idx < n and idx != other:
            hr=h[idx]
            if hr: Q[d] -= st
            else: P[d] += st
    e=b; st=nb-ob; other=a
    for d in range(1,n):
       ...
    # pair
    d=abs(a-b)
    if a < b:
       old_l=oa; old_r=ob; new_l=na; new_r=nb
    else: ...
    oldP=1 if old_l and not old_r else 0
    oldQ=1 if not old_l and old_r else 0
    newP=1 if new_l and not new_r else 0
    newQ=1 if not new_l and new_r else 0
    P[d] += newP - oldP
    Q[d] += newQ - oldQ
    h[a]=na; h[b]=nb
    return max(max(P), max(Q))
```
When endpoint e loop, if idx==other skip. For e=a, if other b is at e±d, skip; pair handles. For e=b skip a. Good.

For st = -1 (1->0): left L=1: P[d] -= -1 => P++? Let's re-evaluate: endpoint e old 1 new0 (st=-1). If r<e (e right), L fixed. If L=1, old pair 1-1 -> no P; new 1-0 -> P. deltaP=+1. Code L=1: P[d] -= st = -(-1)=+1 good. If L=0, old 0-1 -> Q; new 0-0 -> no. deltaQ=-1. Code else Q += st = -1 good. If e left R fixed: R=0 old 1-0 P, new0-0 no => P -1. Code else P += st = -1 good. R=1 old 1-1 none, new0-1 Q => Q +1. Code if R: Q -= st = +1 good.
For st=+1 (0->1): e right L=1: old1-0 P, new1-1 no -> P -1; code P -= +1 = -1 good. L=0: old none, new0-1 Q +1; code Q += +1. e left R=0: old none, new1-0 P +1; P += +1. R=1: old 0-1 Q, new1-1 none -> Q -1; Q -= +1. Good.

Need ensure P/Q values don't go negative; if they do due bug, max maybe. Could after many iterations recompute full to correct: `if iter_count % 10000 ==0: P,Q,m = init_binary_state(h); cur_m=m`. This also corrects. Good.

Let's design binary_search_n more concretely:
```
def binary_search_n(n, allowed, parent, seed, time_limit):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_seq = None; best_m = 10**9
    # If no parent, start random
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
       th = threshold_parent_to_binary(n, parent, rng)
       starts.append(th)
    for _ in range(3): starts.append(run_generator(n, allowed, rng))
    for _ in range(2): starts.append(random_binary(n, rng))
    for alpha in [0.31,0.37,0.43,0.47,0.53]: starts.append(rotation_binary(n, alpha, rng))
    # Limit starts to fit time: each start time slice = max(5, time_limit/(len(starts)+2))
    for seq in starts:
       if time_limit - elapsed < 8: break
       P,Q,m = init_binary_state(h_copy)
       # quick hill without SA for 1-2s? SA will.
       m, h = binary_hill_sa(... time_slice, target_allowed=allowed)
       if m < best_m: best...
       if best_m <= allowed and best_m <= 0.9*allowed? maybe continue only if time.
    # If still not enough? We can do one long SA from best_seq with remaining.
    if time left > 20: continue from best_seq.
    return best_seq,best_m
```
But implementing continue from best_seq requires P/Q reinit. Could write `improve_binary(start_h, P,Q,m, time, allowed, rng, best_m,...)` returns improved. For initial starts, use same.

Algorithm per n:
1. Generate K=20 start sequences (threshold, runs, random, rotation).
2. For each, compute initial m (init state). Keep top maybe 3 by m.
3. For top starts (or all if time), run SA for time slice. Track best.
4. If best_m <= allowed, maybe accept but can try top starts more.
5. Return best.

Starting with many quick inits O(K*N^2) trivial. This avoids wasting SA on bad starts.

Let's implement run generator robust.
```
def random_composition(total, parts, max_len, rng):
    if parts <=0: return []
    if total < parts: # impossible
       parts = total
       lengths = [1]*parts
       return lengths
    lengths = [1]*parts
    rem = total - parts
    max_len = max(max_len, (total + parts -1)//parts + 1)
    # distribute rem in chunks to avoid many loops if rem large; use while rem>0
    while rem > 0:
       # choose up to chunk random runs
       # probability proportional to remaining capacity maybe
       avail = [i for i,l in enumerate(lengths) if l < max_len]
       if avail:
          idx = avail[rng.randint(len(avail))]
       else:
          idx = rng.randint(parts)
       # add 1 or more? For speed, add up to min(rem, max_len - lengths[idx]) maybe.
       add = min(rem, max_len - lengths[idx])
       if add <=0: add=1
       lengths[idx] += add
       rem -= add
    return lengths
```
But adding in chunks uniform can make distribution skewed but okay. To get run lengths 2-3, max_len 6. For total50 parts19 rem31, chunk maybe up to4; average. Good.
`max_len` choose maybe 7. If parts small, rem large, max_len must allow. If parts=18, total50, max_len at least ceil(50/18)=3.

Run generator:
```
def generate_run_binary(n, allowed, rng):
    half=n//2
    # choose number of 1-runs between max(2,int(0.7*allowed)) and min(half-1,int(1.3*allowed)+3)
    R1_low = max(2, int(0.7*allowed)-2)
    R1_high = min(half-1, int(1.3*allowed)+3)
    if R1_high < R1_low: R1=R1_high=R1_low=...
    R1 = rng.randint(R1_low, R1_high+1)
    R0_choices = [R1-1,R1,R1+1] subset 1..half-1? zeros runs at least1.
    R0 = rng.choice([x for x in [R1-1,R1,R1+1] if 1<=x<=half-1]) 
    start_one = rng.rand()<0.5
    max_len = rng.randint(4,8)
    ones_lengths = random_composition(half, R1, max_len, rng)
    zeros_lengths = random_composition(n-half, R0, max_len, rng)
    seq=[]
    if start_one:
       # alternate O,Z starting O. Number runs total max(R1,R0)
       idx=0
       for r in range(max(R1,R0)):
          if r < R1: seq.extend([1]*ones_lengths[r])
          if r < R0: seq.extend([0]*zeros_lengths[r])
       # This creates O Z O Z ... If R0=R1+1 and starts O, last bits? sequence O Z O Z ... Z, okay.
       # But if R0=R1-1, last one? okay.
    else:
       for r in range(max(R1,R0)):
          if r < R0: seq.extend([0]*zeros_lengths[r])
          if r < R1: seq.extend([1]*ones_lengths[r])
    # length n. Ensure sum half by construction. But if max_len distribution leaves? comps exact.
    if len(seq)!=n:
       # fallback random exact
       return random_binary(n,rng)
    return seq
```
Wait if start_one and R0=R1-1: sequence O Z O Z ... O (ends one). Alternation okay. P1 (1->0) = R1-1? Q1=R1-1? Not exactly. But roughly.

We need max_len not too high; if composition rem > capacity, max_len adjusted to allow length. Good.

Rotation binary:
```
def rotation_binary(n, alpha, rng):
    phase = rng.random()
    vals=np.array([ (i*alpha + phase) % 1.0 for i in range(n)])
    # threshold top half
    thr=np.median(vals)
    arr=(vals>thr).astype(int)
    # if count != half adjust by sorting actual vals
    cnt=sum arr; if cnt>half: order=np.argsort(vals)[half:]; arr[order]=0? Need maintain top half.
```
Simpler: `arr=np.zeros(n,int); order=np.argsort(vals); arr[order[-half:]]=1`. This preserves rotation regions. For rotation with threshold 0.5, top half may be same as threshold plus boundary adjustment.

Random binary exact:
```
def random_binary(n, rng):
    arr=np.zeros(n,int); idx=rng.choice(n, replace=False, size=n//2, shuffle=False); arr[idx]=1; return arr.tolist()
```

Threshold parent:
```
def threshold_parent_binary(n, parent, rng):
    p=np.asarray(parent, float); np_old=len(p)
    x_old=np.linspace(0,2,np_old,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    vals=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
    # add tiny noise to break ties
    vals += rng.random(n)*1e-9
    order=np.argsort(vals)
    arr=np.zeros(n,int); arr[order[-n//2:]]=1
    return arr.tolist()
```
Use rng for ties? We can just add noise.

Initial state function:
```
def init_binary_state(h):
    n=len(h)
    P=[0]*n; Q=[0]*n
    # maybe use for i,j
    for i in range(n):
       hi=h[i]
       if hi:
          # need j>i where h[j]=0
          for j in range(i+1,n):
             if h[j]==0: P[j-i]+=1
       else:
          for j in range(i+1,n):
             if h[j]==1: Q[j-i]+=1
    m=max(max(P),max(Q))
    return P,Q,m
```
O(n^2) Python loops; n=160 ~25k, K=30 =>750k loops fine. Could vectorize: for k in range(1,n): `P[k]=np.dot(np.array(h)[:-k], ... )`; but h list convert. Python loops okay. But `h[j]` inside nested maybe 25k*? fine.

Could initialize P,Q using formula faster with numpy: `hnp=np.array(h,dtype=int); for k: P[k]=np.sum(hnp[:-k]*(1-hnp[k:]))`. O(n^2) in C but loop n (160), fine. Simpler and less bug. Use that for init; update uses lists. For h list, convert to np each init.
```
def init_binary_state(h):
   n=len(h); hnp=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.int64)
   P=[0]*n; Q=[0]*n
   for k in range(1,n):
      P[k]=int(np.dot(hnp[:-k], 1-hnp[k:]))
      Q[k]=int(np.dot(hnp[k:], 1-hnp[:-k]))
   m=max(max(P),max(Q))
   return P,Q,m
```
This uses n-1 vector ops per init, K=30,n=160 =>4800 vector ops, okay. For continuous maybe similar.

For binary SA, h as list; P/Q as list. Need convert selected seq from list.

SLSQP needs objective/jac top-level; set globals before call.
```
_SMOOTH_BETA=1.0
_SMOOTH_TARGET=0.0
def smooth_objective(h):
   return smooth_max_value(h, _SMOOTH_BETA)
def smooth_gradient(h):
   return smooth_max_grad(h, _SMOOTH_BETA)
def sum_obj(h):
   return np.sum(h) - _SMOOTH_TARGET
def sum_jac(h):
   return np.ones(len(h))
```

`smooth_max_value`:
```
def smooth_max_value(h,beta):
    P,Q=cont_dir_vals(h)
    vals=np.concatenate((P,Q[1:]))
    m=vals.max()
    z=np.clip(beta*(vals-m), -60, 60)
    s=np.sum(np.exp(z))
    return (m + np.log(s)/beta)*(2.0/len(h))
```
For gradient, need weights. Recompute P,Q, vals, z, s. Then:
```
g=np.zeros(n); total_s=s
# P weights z[:n]
for k in range(n):
   w=np.exp(z[k])/total_s
   if w>1e-10: g += w*grad_P(k,h)
# Q weights z[n:] correspond k=1..n-1
for idx,k in enumerate(range(1,n)):
   w=...
   if w>1e-10: g += w*grad_Q(k,h)
return g*(2.0/len(h))
```

Pseudo:
```
def lp_refine_float(h, budget_s, radius=0.05):
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0; h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    P,Q=cont_dir_vals(h); maxPQ=max(P.max(),Q.max()); maxc=maxPQ*2/n
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()-t0 > budget: break
       # active values: combine P,Q into list of (val, type, k)
       active=[]
       tol=max(1e-6, 0.02*maxPQ)
       for k in range(n): if P[k] >= maxPQ - tol: active.append((P[k],0,k))
       for k in range(1,n): if Q[k] >= maxPQ - tol: active.append((Q[k],1,k))
       # if active too many, sort and take top maybe 60
       if len(active)>60: sort by val desc, keep 60
       # build constraints
       c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1
       A_rows=[]; b_rows=[]
       for val, typ,k in active:
          g = grad_P(k,h) if typ==0 else grad_Q(k,h)
          row=np.empty(n+1); row[:n]=g; row[n]=-1; A_rows.append(row); b_rows.append(-val)
       A=np.vstack(A_rows)
       b=np.array(b_rows)
       Aeq=np.ones(n+1); Aeq[-1]=0; beq=0
       bounds=[(max(-radius, -hi), min(radius,1-hi)) for hi in h] + [(0,None)]
       try res=linprog(c,A,b,Aeq,Aeq,bounds,method='highs')
       except:
          # subgradient step
       if res.status==0:
          d=res.x[:n]
          base=maxc
          best=base; best_h=h
          for th in (1,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
             cand=project_box_sum(h+th*d,target)
             P2,Q2=cont_dir_vals(cand); m2=max(...)*2/n
             if m2<best-1e-12: best=m2; best_h=cand
          if best < maxc - 1e-12:
             h=best_h; maxc=best; P,Q=cont_dir_vals(h); maxPQ=maxc*n/2
          else:
             # if LP direction not improving, try smooth gradient step or break
             ...
       else: use subgradient
    return h,maxc
```

def quantize_exact(h, n, denom=16):
    target_units = round((n/2.0)*denom) # integer
    vals = np.rint(h*denom).astype(int)
    vals = np.clip(vals,0,denom)
    surplus = target_units - vals.sum()
    # adjust by adding/subtracting units to entries not at bound, sorted by distance?
    if surplus>0:
       order=np.argsort(-vals)?? Wait need increase values; choose entries with smallest distance to next integer? For adding 1, choose smallest fractional (h*denom - vals) maybe.
       # simpler loop: while surplus>0: idx=np.argmax(vals<denom); vals[idx]+=1
    elif surplus<0: while ... idx=np.argmin(vals>0); vals[idx]-=1
    # If loop too many, distribute
    return vals/denom
```

Let's code SLSQP smooth:
```
def run_slsqp_smooth(h0, beta, maxiter, ftol, target):
    global _SM_BETA, _SM_TARGET
    _SM_BETA = float(beta); _SM_TARGET = float(target)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*len(h0)
    constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sm_sum,'jac':sm_sum_jac}]
    res=None
    try:
       res=minimize(sm_fun,h0,method='SLSQP',jac=sm_jac,bounds=bounds,constraints=constraints,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol})
    except Exception:
       pass
    if res is not None and res.x is not None:
       return project_box_sum(res.x,target)
    return h0
```
Potential issue: `scipy.optimize.minimize` with SLSQP and `jac=sm_jac` where sm_jac uses global beta. Good.

Custom smooth value/grad uses dx. SLSQP objective value at h might be close to max* dx plus smooth bias. Good.

def binary_improve(h, P, Q, m, time_limit, rng, goal_m=None):
    h=h.copy(); P=P.copy(); Q=Q.copy(); cur_m=m; best_m=m; best_h=h.copy(); best_P? no need.
    start_t=time.time(); end=start_t+time_limit
    no_improve_count=0; max_no=5000? But with SA, no matter.
    T0=1.0; Tend=0.05
    iter_no=0
    # optional initial greedy: for first 1000 steps only accept improvement
    while time.time()<end:
       # schedule T
       frac=(time.time()-start_t)/time_limit if time_limit>0 else 1
       T=max(Tend, T0*(Tend/T0)**frac)
       if goal_m is not None and best_m <= goal_m:
           # try to lower, use SA with lower T maybe
           pass
       # choose a,b diff
       while True: a=rng.randint(n); b=rng.randint(n); if a!=b and h[a]!=h[b]: break
       new_m=binary_swap(h,P,Q,a,b)
       accept=False
       if new_m < cur_m: accept=True
       elif new_m == cur_m: accept = (rng.rand()<0.2)  # allow moves equal to escape? But equal can wander; maybe low prob.
       else:
           delta=new_m-cur_m
           if delta <= 6*T and rng.rand() < np.exp(-delta/T): accept=True
       if accept:
           cur_m=new_m
           if new_m < best_m:
               best_m=new_m; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
           else: no_improve+=1
       else:
           cur_m=binary_swap(h,P,Q,a,b)  # revert
           no_improve+=1
       iter_no +=1
       if iter_no % 20000 ==0:
           # recompute to correct, and maybe recenter SA T? Also if best_h not current.
           P,Q,cur_m = init_binary_state(h)
       if best_m <= goal_m and rng logic? Not.
    return best_h,best_m

```
def balanced_run_binary(n, target, rng):
   half=n//2
   R1 = max(2,min(half-1, target + rng.randint(-2,3)))
   R0_choices...
   def composition_balanced(total,parts, rng):
      q, r = divmod(total, parts)
      lengths=[q]*parts
      # add 1 to random r positions; if q=0 not allowed but parts<=total
      idx=rng.choice(parts, replace=False, size=r) if r>0 else []
      for i in idx: lengths[i]+=1
      return lengths
   # if q==0? parts>total, reduce parts.
```
This gives very uniform run lengths. For half50 R1=18 -> lengths 2/3. Good. We can use instead of random_composition as better inits. For each start, randomize R1 and placement.

def binary_search_n(n, goal_m, parent, seed, time_limit):
    start=time.time(); end=start+time_limit
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    allowed = max(2, goal_m)
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None: starts.append(threshold_parent_binary(n,parent,rng))
    for _ in range(4):
        rr=generate_run_binary(n, allowed, rng)
        starts.append(rr)
    for _ in range(2): starts.append(random_binary(n,rng))
    for alpha in [0.31,0.37,0.43,0.53]:
        starts.append(rotation_binary(n, alpha, rng))
    # evaluate initial
    evaluated=[]
    for seq in starts:
       if len(seq)!=n or sum(seq)!=n/2:
          seq=random_binary(n,rng)
       P,Q,m=init_binary_state(seq)
       evaluated.append((m,seq))
    evaluated.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
```

Now, continuous `project_box_sum`: Need ensure target n/2 can be .5 for odd. Projection with bounds continuous. For n odd, sum target .5; values continuous. Bisection fine. Implementation:
```
def project_box_sum(x,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    n=len(x)
    # λ such that sum clip(x-λ,0,1)=target
    lo = x.min() - 1.0
    hi = x.max() + 1.0? Wait for large λ, sum0; for λ small, sum n. We need lo where sum>=target, hi where sum<=target.
    # set lo = min(x) - 1 (x-λ >=1? if λ <= min-1 => all >=1? Not all 1 if x differ, but sum>=target likely). hi=max(x)+1.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.sum(np.clip(x-mid,0,1))
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    diff=target-np.sum(y)
    # fix on free
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          free=(y<1.0-1e-12)
          if np.any(free): y[free]+= diff/np.sum(free); y=np.clip(y,0,1)
       else:
          free=(y>1e-12)
          if np.any(free): y[free]+= diff/np.sum(free); y=np.clip(y,0,1)
    return y
```

def quantize_exact(h, denom=32):
    n=len(h); target_units = int(round(float(n)*denom/2.0))
    u = np.rint(np.asarray(h,float)*denom).astype(int)
    u = np.clip(u,0,denom)
    surplus = target_units - int(u.sum())
    if surplus > 0:
       # add to entries with most fractional part or smallest distance to next integer
       for _ in range(surplus):
          cand = np.where(u < denom)[0]
          if len(cand)==0: break
          # choose one with largest h*denom - u? (closest to next unit)
          frac = np.asarray(h)*denom - u
          frac[cand ==?]? Need only cand. Simpler: scores = np.where(u<denom, frac, -1); idx=np.argmax(scores); u[idx]+=1
    elif surplus < 0:
       for _ in range(-surplus):
          cand = np.where(u > 0)[0]
          if len(cand)==0: break
          # choose one with smallest h*denom - u (closest to previous unit) i.e. most fractional? For reducing, choose largest u - h*denom? Actually if h*denom=3.9, u=4, reduce to3, increase maybe? To minimize change, choose u - floor? We want lower; choose candidate with smallest fractional part (h*denom - floor?) If u=4 and value 4.1, reduce to4? no. Choose u with smallest distance to u-1: (value - (u-1)) large? Simpler: choose one with smallest above? Scores.
          # Use distance to u: val_h*denom; reduce u by1, new error = |val - (u-1)| - |val-u|. Choose minimal.
```

`lp_refine`:
```
def lp_refine(h, time_limit, radius=0.05):
    n=len(h); target=n/2.0; t_start=time.time()
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    P,Q=cont_dir_vals(h); maxv=max(P.max(),Q.max()); maxc=maxv*2/n
    no_improve=0
    while time.time()-t_start < time_limit and no_improve<8:
       active=[]
       tol=max(1e-8,0.03*maxv)
       for k in range(n):
          if P[k] >= maxv - tol: active.append((P[k],0,k))
       for k in range(1,n):
          if Q[k] >= maxv - tol: active.append((Q[k],1,k))
       # sort by val
       active.sort(reverse=True) # sorts by val then type? tuple (val, type, k); reverse okay; type tie not issue
       if len(active)>80: active=active[:80]
       rows=[]; bs=[]
       for val,typ,k in active:
           g=cont_grad_P(k,h) if typ==0 else cont_grad_Q(k,h)
           row=np.empty(n+1); row[:n]=g; row[n]=-1.0; rows.append(row); bs.append(-float(val))
       if not rows: break
       c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
       A=np.vstack(rows); b=np.array(bs)
       Aeq=np.ones(n+1); Aeq[n]=0.0; beq=0.0
       bounds_list=[]
       for val in h:
          lows=-radius if -radius > -val else -val? Actually max(-radius, -val)
          up=radius if radius < 1-val else 1-val
          bounds_list.append((lows, up))
       bounds_list.append((0.0, None))
       res=None
       try: res=linprog(c,A,b,Aeq.beq,bounds_list,method='highs')
       except Exception: pass
       improved=False
       if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False):
          d=res.x[:n]
          best=maxc; best_h=h
          for theta in (1.0,0.6,0.35,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
              cand=project_box_sum(h + theta*d, target)
              Pc,Qc=cont_dir_vals(cand)
              mc=max(Pc.max(),Qc.max())*2/n
              if mc < best - 1e-12:
                  best=mc; best_h=cand
          if best < maxc - 1e-12:
              h=best_h; maxc=best; no_improve=0; continue
       # fallback smooth descent step
       gval=smooth_max_grad(h, 20.0)
       # gval already C units. Try negative steps
       best=maxc; best_h=h
       lr=0.02
       for _ in range(4):
          cand=project_box_sum(h - lr*gval, target)
          Pc,Qc=cont_dir_vals(cand); mc=max(...)*2/n
          if mc<best-1e-12: best=mc; best_h=cand
          lr *=0.5
       if best < maxc -1e-12: h=best_h; maxc=best; no_improve=0
       else: no_improve +=1
    h=project_box_sum(h,target); Pc,Qc=...; maxc...
    return h,maxc
```

def continuous_refine(hstart, n, budget_s, current_best):
    t=time.time(); end=t+budget_s
    h=np.array(hstart,float); h=project_box_sum(h,n/2)
    c=compute_c5(h)
    # If already below current? proceed maybe to improve.
    if budget_s > 20:
       # beta list based on n
       betas=[(10,40),(30,30),(80,20)]
       for beta,maxiter in betas:
           if time.time() > end - 5: break
           h2=run_slsqp_smooth(h,beta,maxiter,1e-10)
           c2=compute_c5(h2)
           if c2 < c - 1e-12:
              h=h2; c=c2
    # LP with remaining
    rem=end-time.time()
    if rem > 10:
       h,c = lp_refine(h, min(60, rem*0.9), 0.03)
    # quantize
    hq=quantize_exact(h,32)
    cq=compute_c5(hq)
    if cq < c and cq < current_best? Need return only if cq<current_best? run will check. If cq >= c maybe return h,c? But not exact? We'll return hq,cq if exact; run checks. If hq decreases c? quantization might increase. We can keep h if sum close? To be safe return hq,cq.
    return hq,cq
```

Detailed `binary_search_n`:
```
def binary_search_n(n, goal_m, parent, seed, time_limit):
    start_t = time.time()
    end_t = start_t + max(1.0, time_limit)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    half = n // 2
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
       starts.append(threshold_parent_binary(n, parent, rng))
    build_m = max(3, goal_m)
    for _ in range(5):
       starts.append(generate_run_binary(n, build_m, rng))
    for _ in range(2): starts.append(random_binary(n, rng))
    for alpha in (0.31,0.37,0.43,0.53): starts.append(rotation_binary(n, alpha, rng))
    evaluated=[]
    for idx, seq in enumerate(starts):
       if len(seq) != n or sum(seq) != half:
           seq = random_binary(n, rng)
       P,Q,m = init_binary_state(seq)
       evaluated.append((m, idx, seq))
    evaluated.sort()
    if not evaluated:
       seq = random_binary(n,rng); P,Q,m=init...; return seq,m
    best_m = evaluated[0][0]; best_seq = evaluated[0][2]
    top = evaluated[:5]
    for m0, idx0, seq0 in top:
       rem = end_t - time.time()
       if rem < 8: break
       slice_s = min(rem*0.35, max(8.0, time_limit/(len(top)+1)))
       P,Q,m0 = init_binary_state(seq0)
       h1,m1 = binary_improve(seq0, P, Q, m0, slice_s, rng, goal_m)
       if m1 < best_m:
          best_m=m1; best_seq=h1
    rem = end_t - time.time()
    if rem > 10 and best_seq is not None:
       P,Q,m = init_binary_state(best_seq)
       h1,m1 = binary_improve(best_seq, P, Q, m, min(rem*0.95, 400.0), rng, goal_m)
       if m1 < best_m: best_m=m1; best_seq=h1
    return best_seq, best_m
```
This uses goal_m to guide but binary_improve doesn't use to stop; it always tries lowering. It uses goal_m only for temperature? We can modify to use goal_m? Not necessary. We can pass, maybe not used except kick. Could accept equal with low.

Now, `generate_run_binary` details:
```
def generate_run_binary(n, target, rng):
    half = n//2
    low = max(2, int(0.7*target)-2)
    high = min(half-1, int(1.3*target)+4)
    if high < low: high = low
    if low > half-1: low=half-1; high=low
    if low < 1: low=1
    if high < 1: high=1
    R1 = int(rng.randint(low, high+1))
    choices=[R1-1,R1,R1+1]
    valid=[r for r in choices if 1 <= r <= half-1]
    if not valid: valid=[R1]
    R0=int(rng.choice(valid))
    max_len = int(rng.randint(4,8))
    ol = random_composition(half, R1, max_len, rng)
    zl = random_composition(half, R0, max_len, rng)
    start_one = bool(rng.rand() < 0.5)
    seq=[]
    if start_one:
       total_runs=max(R1,R0)
       for r in range(total_runs):
          if r < R1: seq.extend([1]*ol[r])
          if r < R0: seq.extend([0]*zl[r])
    else:
       total_runs=max(R1,R0)
       for r in range(total_runs):
          if r < R0: seq.extend([0]*zl[r])
          if r < R1: seq.extend([1]*ol[r])
    if len(seq) != n or sum(seq) != half:
       return random_binary(n,rng)
    return seq
```
Potential if R0=0? valid ensures >=1. half-1 at least? n even >=? We'll not use n<8.

`random_composition`: If total<parts, increase total? In generate, parts low<=half-1, total half, so ok.
```
def random_composition(total, parts, max_len, rng):
    if parts <= 0: return []
    if total < parts:
        total = parts
    lengths=[1]*parts
    rem=total-parts
    max_len=max(max_len, int((total + parts -1)//parts) + 1)
    while rem > 0:
       # find available up to max_len
       avail=[]
       for i,l in enumerate(lengths):
          if l < max_len:
             avail.append(i)
       if avail:
          idx=avail[int(rng.randint(len(avail)))]
          add=rem
          cap=max_len-lengths[idx]
          if add>cap: add=cap
          if add < 1: add=1
       else:
          idx=int(rng.randint(parts))
          add=1
          if lengths[idx] >= max_len: add=rem # allow exceed
       lengths[idx] += add
       rem -= add
    return lengths
```
This can add many at once causing longer runs but okay. To get distribution, maybe add 1 at a time when rem large? Loop rem up to 50, fine. Use add=1 for better random distribution. Let's set add=1 unless rem < cap. Simpler:
```
while rem>0:
   avail = [i for i,l in enumerate(lengths) if l < max_len]
   if avail: idx=...
   else: idx=rng.randint(parts)
   lengths[idx]+=1; rem-=1
```
rem up to 100, fine. This uniform distribution.

`random_composition` list comprehension `avail=[...]` okay. No lambda.

`rotation_binary`: Need use top half to ensure count.
```
def rotation_binary(n, alpha, rng):
    phase=float(rng.random())
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    vals=(x*alpha + phase) % 1.0
    order=np.argsort(vals)
    arr=np.zeros(n, dtype=int)
    arr[order[-n//2:]] = 1
    return arr.tolist()
```
For alpha near 0.5, random. Good.

`threshold_parent_binary`: If parent length maybe 0. Need handle parent None. Use interpolation.
```
def threshold_parent_binary(n,parent,rng):
    p=np.asarray(parent,float).ravel()
    m=len(p)
    if m ==0: return random_binary(n,rng)
    if m == n:
       vals=p.copy()
    else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m,endpoint=False)
       x_new=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
       vals=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    # Add small deterministic noise? rng
    vals = vals + rng.random(n)*1e-8
    order=np.argsort(vals)
    arr=np.zeros(n,dtype=int); arr[order[-n//2:]]=1
    return arr.tolist()
```

def continuous_refine(hstart,n,budget_s,current_best):
    tstart=time.time(); end=tstart+budget_s
    h=np.array(hstart, dtype=float)
    target=float(n)/2.0
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=compute_c5(h)
    # SLSQP
    if budget_s > 25:
       if n <=100:
          schedule=[(5.0,50),(20.0,40),(60.0,30),(150.0,25)]
       else:
          schedule=[(10.0,30),(40.0,25),(100.0,20)]
       for beta,maxiter in schedule:
          rem=end-time.time()
          if rem < 10: break
          h2=run_slsqp_smooth(h, beta, maxiter, 1e-10)
          if h2 is not None:
             c2=compute_c5(h2)
             if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                h=h2; c=c2
    rem=end-time.time()
    if rem > 8:
       h,c = lp_refine(h, min(80.0, rem*0.8), 0.03)
    # final project
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    c=compute_c5(h)
    # quantize
    hq=quantize_exact(h, 32)
    cq=compute_c5(hq)
    if cq < c:
       h=hq; c=cq
    return h,c
```

`quantize_exact` implementation with denom=64:
```
def quantize_exact(h, denom):
    n=len(h)
    target_units = int(round(float(n) * denom / 2.0))
    val = np.asarray(h,float) * float(denom)
    u = np.rint(val).astype(int)
    u = np.clip(u,0,denom)
    surplus = target_units - int(np.sum(u))
    if surplus > 0:
       for _ in range(surplus):
          mask = u < denom
          if not np.any(mask): break
          score = val.copy()
          score[~mask] = -1e100
          # choose largest val-u? Use score = val - u
          score = val - u
          score[~mask] = -1e100
          idx = int(np.argmax(score))
          u[idx] += 1
    elif surplus < 0:
       neg=-surplus
       for _ in range(neg):
          mask = u > 0
          if not np.any(mask): break
          score = val - u
          score[~mask] = 1e100
          idx = int(np.argmin(score))
          u[idx] -= 1
    return u.astype(float) / float(denom)
```
`val.copy()` unnecessary. For surplus<0, choose minimal score (most negative, i.e., val far below u, closer to u-1? Example val=3.1,u=3 score0.1; val=3.9,u=4 score-0.1; choose 3.9 reduce to3 (increase error) vs 3.1 reduce to2 (increase 1.0). Actually we want val closer to u-0.5? Let's not overthink. Sum correction small. Use `score = val - (u-0.5)`? To minimize L1 change, for increase choose largest `val - u`; for decrease choose smallest `u - val`? That's `score = val - u`; for decrease choose largest? Example val=3.9,u=4 score -0.1; val=3.1,u=3 score0.1. We want decrease 3.9? It makes u=3 error0.9 vs current0.1 increase0.8; decrease 3.1 to2 error1.1 increase1.0. So choose smallest score (3.9) actually increases less? Wait current error for 3.9 with u=4 is0.1; new error 0.9 increase0.8. For 3.1 error0.1; new error1.1 increase1.0. So choose smallest score yes. Good. For increase, val=3.1,u=3 score0.1 increase to4 error0.9 inc0.8; val=3.9,u=4 score-0.1 increase to5 error1.1 inc1.0; choose largest score yes. Good.
Need for 0/1, surplus 0.
```
score = val - u
if surplus>0: scores[ u >= denom] = -inf; idx=argmax(score)
else: scores[ u <=0]=inf; idx=argmin(score)
```
Good.

`smooth_max_grad`: To reduce allocations, we can compute grad sum by accumulating from P and Q coefficients directly without full grad arrays. Let's derive more efficient.
For a P_k weighted w:
- Add to g[:n-k] w*(1-h[k:]); add to g[k:] -w*h[:-k].
For Q_k: g[k:] += w*(1-h[:-k]); g[:n-k] -= w*h[k:].
So in gradient loop, we can slice-add, no temp full g per k? Still allocate arrays for slices? Use `g[:n-k] += w*(1.0 - h[k:])` etc. This creates temp vector length n-k. For active all, O(n^2) memory transient but fine. Better than full grad function. We'll implement in `smooth_max_grad` directly.
```
def smooth_max_grad(h,beta):
    n=len(h); P,Q=cont_dir_vals(h)
    vals=np.empty(2*n-1?) # P length n, Q[1:] length n-1 -> 2n-1
    vals[:n]=P; vals[n:]=Q[1:]
    m=vals.max(); z=...; exps=...
    total=exps.sum(); dx=2/n; g=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(n):
       w=exps[k]/total
       if w>1e-10:
          if k==0: g += w*(1-2h)
          else:
             g[:n-k] += w*(1-h[k:])
             g[k:] -= w*h[:-k]
    for idx in range(n-1):
       k=idx+1; w=exps[n+idx]/total
       if w>1e-10:
          g[k:] += w*(1-h[:-k])
          g[:n-k] -= w*h[k:]
    return g*dx
```
This handles k=0. Good.
Similarly `smooth_max_value` can use same vals.

Make sure `vals` length 2n-1 for n>=1. n=1? Not used.

MILP feasibility for target B:
Variables order: A[0:n], Z[n:n+m], Bvar index last.
Objective: minimize Bvar.

- Q_k: sum_{i=k}^{n-1} A_i - sum Z_{i,i+k} - B <= 0.

MIP code:
```
def binary_mip(n, time_limit):
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
       import scipy.sparse as sp
    except Exception:
       return None
    tstart=time.time(); tmax=time_limit
    if tmax <= 1: return None
    half=n//2; m=n*(n-1)//2; total=n+m+1; bvar=n+m
    data=[]; rids=[]; cids=[]; rhs=[]
    # product
    row=0
    for j in range(1,n):
      base=j*(j-1)//2
      for i in range(j):
        z=n+base+i
        data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(z)
        data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(i); rhs.append(0.0); row+=1
        data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(z)
        data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(j); rhs.append(0.0); row+=1
        data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(z)
        data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(i)
        data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(j); rhs.append(-1.0); row+=1
    # P rows
    for k in range(1,n):
      # P
      for i in range(n-k):
         data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(i)
         j=i+k; idx=j*(j-1)//2 + i; data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(n+idx)
      data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(bvar); rhs.append(0.0); row+=1
      # Q
      for i in range(n-k):
         data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(n-k? wait Q uses A_{i+k}=A_j)
```
Q row: sum_{i=0}^{n-1-k} A_{i+k} - Z_i - B ≤0. So for i in range(n-k): A[j] +1, z -1.
```
      for i in range(n-k):
         j=i+k
         data.append(1.0); rows row; cols j
         idx=...; data -1; col z
      data -1 bvar; rhs 0
```
Good.

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+budget_s-5.0
    target=0.38085
    try: parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception: parent=None
    if parent is not None and parent.size==0: parent=None
    if parent is None:
       rng0=np.random.RandomState(seed)
       best_h=np.array(random_binary(120,rng0), dtype=float)
       best_n=120
    else:
       best_h=parent.copy()
       best_n=len(best_h)
    best_c5=compute_c5(best_h)
    best_bin_seq=None
    best_from_binary=False
    if deadline - t0 <= 10:
       return (np.asarray(best_h,float), best_c5, int(best_n))
    # binary
    n_list=[120,100,128,144,160,112,136,148,192]
    reserve_cont = 250.0 if budget_s > 800 else 80.0
    binary_end = deadline - reserve_cont
    idxn=0
    while idxn < len(n_list) and time.time() < binary_end - 30:
       n=int(n_list[idxn]); idxn+=1
       allowed=int(np.floor((target - 1e-12)*float(n)/2.0))
       if allowed < 4: continue
       rem = binary_end - time.time()
       if rem < 45: break
       slice_s = min(240.0, max(45.0, rem*0.30))
       if n > 150: slice_s = min(slice_s, 160.0)
       seq,m = binary_search_n(n, allowed, parent, seed + 1000 + n, slice_s)
       if seq is None: continue
       # verify count
       if sum(seq) != n//2: continue
       hf=np.array(seq,dtype=float)
       c=compute_c5(hf)
       if c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
           best_h=hf; best_c5=c; best_n=n; best_bin_seq=seq; best_from_binary=True
           if best_c5 < target:
               binary_end = deadline - 30.0
    # polish binary if below target
    if best_from_binary and best_c5 < target and time.time() < deadline - 60:
       # get list from best_bin_seq
       hlist=[int(round(float(x))) for x in best_bin_seq]  # list comp no lambda, okay? It's a comprehension not lambda. Allowed? It is fine.
       P,Q,m=init_binary_state(hlist)
       sfr=np.random.RandomState(seed + 7771)
       time_slice=min(300.0, deadline - time.time() - 20.0)
       h2,m2=binary_improve(hlist,P,Q,m,time_slice,sfr,max(1,m-1))
       if m2 < m and m2*2.0/best_n < best_c5 - 1e-12:
           hf=np.array(h2,float); c=compute_c5(hf)
           if c < best_c5: update
    # MIP fallback if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 120:
       # only if best_c5 not very low? We can try small n. Use current best_c5 as limit.
       for n_mip in (64,72,80):
          rem=deadline-time.time()
          if rem < 100: break
          time_slice=min(90.0, rem*0.4)
          seqm,m_max=binary_mip(n_mip, best_c5 if best_c5 < target else target, time_slice)
          if seqm is not None:
              hf=np.array(seqm,float); c=compute_c5(hf)
              if c < best_c5 - 1e-12: update; best_bin_seq=seqm; best_from_binary=True
              # if c < target maybe break? Can try lower n? n smaller maybe. break if time.
    # continuous
    if time.time() < deadline - 60:
       cont_end=deadline
       candidates=[]
       seen=[]
       if parent is not None:
          candidates.append((parent, len(parent)))
          if len(parent) != 100:
             # interpolate helper? We don't have continuous interp function except threshold. Add helper `interp_continuous(n,parent)`.
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    linprog = None

TARGET_C5 = 0.38085

_SM_BETA = 1.0
_SM_TARGET = 0.0


def compute_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return 1.0
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr) * dx)


def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return x
    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(10):
        s = float(np.sum(y))
        diff = float(target) - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            free = y < 1.0 - 1e-12
            if np.any(free):
                y[free] += diff / float(np.sum(free))
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            free = y > 1e-12
            if np.any(free):
                y[free] += diff / float(np.sum(free))
                y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y


def quantize_exact(h, denom=64):
    n = len(h)
    target_units = int(round(float(n) * float(denom) / 2.0))
    val = np.asarray(h, dtype=float) * float(denom)
    u = np.rint(val).astype(int)
    u = np.clip(u, 0, denom)
    surplus = target_units - int(np.sum(u))
    if surplus > 0:
        for _ in range(surplus):
            mask = u < denom
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            score = val - u.astype(float)
            score[~mask] = -1e100
            idx = int(np.argmax(score))
            u[idx] += 1
    elif surplus < 0:
        for _ in range(-surplus):
            mask = u > 0
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            score = val - u.astype(float)
            score[~mask] = 1e100
            idx = int(np.argmin(score))
            u[idx] -= 1
    return u.astype(float) / float(denom)


def random_binary(n, rng):
    arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=int)
    if n > 0:
        idx = rng.choice(n, replace=False, size=n // 2, shuffle=False)
        arr[idx] = 1
    return arr.tolist()


def random_composition(total, parts, max_len, rng):
    if parts <= 0:
        return []
    total = max(int(total), int(parts))
    parts = int(parts)
    lengths = [1] * parts
    rem = total - parts
    max_len = max(int(max_len), int((total + parts - 1) // parts) + 1)
    while rem > 0:
        avail = []
        for i, lval in enumerate(lengths):
            if lval < max_len:
                avail.append(i)
        if avail:
            idx = avail[int(rng.randint(len(avail)))]
            lengths[idx] += 1
            rem -= 1
        else:
            idx = int(rng.randint(parts))
            lengths[idx] += 1
            rem -= 1
    return lengths


def generate_run_binary(n, target, rng):
    half = n // 2
    low = max(2, int(0.70 * target) - 2)
    high = min(half - 1, int(1.30 * target) + 4)
    if high < low:
        high = low
    if low > half - 1:
        low = half - 1
        high = low
    if low < 1:
        low = 1
    if high < 1:
        high = 1
    R1 = int(rng.randint(low, high + 1))
    choices = [R1 - 1, R1, R1 + 1]
    valid = []
    for r in choices:
        if 1 <= r <= half - 1:
            valid.append(r)
    if not valid:
        valid = [R1]
    R0 = int(rng.choice(valid))
    max_len = int(rng.randint(4, 8))
    one_lens = random_composition(half, R1, max_len, rng)
    zero_lens = random_composition(half, R0, max_len, rng)
    start_one = bool(rng.rand() < 0.5)
    seq = []
    if start_one:
        total_runs = max(R1, R0)
        for r in range(total_runs):
            if r < R1:
                seq.extend([1] * one_lens[r])
            if r < R0:
                seq.extend([0] * zero_lens[r])
    else:
        total_runs = max(R1, R0)
        for r in range(total_runs):
            if r < R0:
                seq.extend([0] * zero_lens[r])
            if r < R1:
                seq.extend([1] * one_lens[r])
    if len(seq) != n or sum(seq) != half:
        return random_binary(n, rng)
    return seq


def rotation_binary(n, alpha, rng):
    phase = float(rng.random())
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    vals = (x * float(alpha) + phase) % 1.0
    order = np.argsort(vals)
    arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=int)
    arr[order[-n // 2:]] = 1
    return arr.tolist()


def interp_continuous_to_n(n, parent):
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = len(p)
    if m == n:
        return p.copy()
    if m == 0:
        return np.full(n, 0.5)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)


def threshold_parent_binary(n, parent, rng):
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = len(p)
    if m == 0:
        return random_binary(n, rng)
    if m == n:
        vals = p.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        vals = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    vals = vals + rng.random(n) * 1e-8
    order = np.argsort(vals)
    arr = np.zeros(n, dtype=int)
    arr[order[-n // 2:]] = 1
    return arr.tolist()


def init_binary_state(h):
    n = len(h)
    hnp = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int64)
    P = [0] * n
    Q = [0] * n
    for k in range(1, n):
        P[k] = int(np.dot(hnp[:-k], 1 - hnp[k:]))
        Q[k] = int(np.dot(hnp[k:], 1 - hnp[:-k]))
    m = max(max(P), max(Q))
    return P, Q, int(m)


def binary_swap(h, P, Q, a, b):
    n = len(h)
    oa = h[a]
    ob = h[b]
    na = 1 - oa
    nb = 1 - ob

    e = a
    st = na - oa
    other = b
    for d in range(1, n):
        idx = e - d
        if idx >= 0 and idx != other:
            hr = h[idx]
            if hr:
                P[d] -= st
            else:
                Q[d] += st
        idx = e + d
        if idx < n and idx != other:
            hr = h[idx]
            if hr:
                Q[d] -= st
            else:
                P[d] += st

    e = b
    st = nb - ob
    other = a
    for d in range(1, n):
        idx = e - d
        if idx >= 0 and idx != other:
            hr = h[idx]
            if hr:
                P[d] -= st
            else:
                Q[d] += st
        idx = e + d
        if idx < n and idx != other:
            hr = h[idx]
            if hr:
                Q[d] -= st
            else:
                P[d] += st

    d = abs(a - b)
    if a < b:
        oL, oR = oa, ob
        nL, nR = na, nb
    else:
        oL, oR = ob, oa
        nL, nR = nb, na

    oldP = 1 if (oL and (not oR)) else 0
    oldQ = 1 if ((not oL) and oR) else 0
    newP = 1 if (nL and (not nR)) else 0
    newQ = 1 if ((not nL) and nR) else 0
    P[d] += newP - oldP
    Q[d] += newQ - oldQ

    h[a] = na
    h[b] = nb
    return int(max(max(P), max(Q)))


def binary_improve(h, P, Q, m, time_limit, rng, goal_m):
    h = h[:]
    P = P[:]
    Q = Q[:]
    n = len(h)
    start_t = time.time()
    end_t = start_t + max(1e-6, float(time_limit))
    cur_m = int(m)
    best_m = cur_m
    best_h = h[:]
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    T0 = 1.0
    Tend = 0.03

    while time.time() < end_t:
        frac = (time.time() - start_t) / (end_t - start_t)
        if frac < 0.0:
            frac = 0.0
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0
        T = max(Tend, T0 * (Tend / T0) ** frac)

        a = -1
        b = -1
        chosen = False
        for _ in range(25):
            aa = int(rng.randint(n))
            bb = int(rng.randint(n))
            if aa != bb and h[aa] != h[bb]:
                a = aa
                b = bb
                chosen = True
                break
        if not chosen:
            a1 = -1
            b1 = -1
            for i in range(n):
                if h[i] == 1 and a1 < 0:
                    a1 = i
                elif h[i] == 0 and b1 < 0:
                    b1 = i
            if a1 < 0 or b1 < 0:
                break
            a = a1
            b = b1

        new_m = binary_swap(h, P, Q, a, b)
        accept = False
        if new_m < cur_m:
            accept = True
        elif new_m == cur_m:
            accept = bool(rng.rand() < (0.03 if frac > 0.80 else 0.10))
        else:
            if frac <= 0.80:
                delta = float(new_m - cur_m)
                if delta <= 50.0 * T:
                    if rng.rand() < float(np.exp(-delta / T)):
                        accept = True

        if accept:
            cur_m = new_m
            if new_m < best_m:
                best_m = new_m
                best_h = h[:]
            no_improve += 1
        else:
            cur_m = binary_swap(h, P, Q, a, b)
            no_improve += 1

        it += 1
        if no_improve > 7000 and frac < 0.80:
            T = max(T, 0.8)
            no_improve = 0
        if it % 20000 == 0:
            P, Q, cur_m = init_binary_state(h)
            if cur_m < best_m:
                best_m = cur_m
                best_h = h[:]

    return best_h, best_m


def binary_search_n(n, goal_m, parent, seed, time_limit):
    start_t = time.time()
    end_t = start_t + max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    half = n // 2
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(threshold_parent_binary(n, parent, rng))
    build_m = max(3, int(goal_m))
    for _ in range(5):
        starts.append(generate_run_binary(n, build_m, rng))
    for _ in range(2):
        starts.append(random_binary(n, rng))
    for alpha in (0.31, 0.37, 0.43, 0.53):
        starts.append(rotation_binary(n, alpha, rng))

    evaluated = []
    for idx, seq in enumerate(starts):
        if len(seq) != n or sum(seq) != half:
            seq = random_binary(n, rng)
        P, Q, m = init_binary_state(seq)
        evaluated.append((m, idx, seq))
    evaluated.sort()
    if not evaluated:
        seq = random_binary(n, rng)
        P, Q, m = init_binary_state(seq)
        return seq, m

    best_m = int(evaluated[0][0])
    best_seq = evaluated[0][2][:]
    top = evaluated[:5]

    for m0, idx0, seq0 in top:
        rem = end_t - time.time()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        slice_s = min(rem * 0.35, max(8.0, float(time_limit) / float(len(top) + 1)))
        P, Q, m0 = init_binary_state(seq0)
        h1, m1 = binary_improve(seq0, P, Q, m0, slice_s, rng, goal_m)
        if m1 < best_m:
            best_m = m1
            best_seq = h1

    rem = end_t - time.time()
    if rem > 10.0 and best_seq is not None:
        P, Q, m = init_binary_state(best_seq)
        h1, m1 = binary_improve(best_seq, P, Q, m, min(rem * 0.95, 400.0), rng, goal_m)
        if m1 < best_m:
            best_m = m1
            best_seq = h1

    return best_seq, best_m


def binary_mip(n, limit_c5, time_limit):
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
        import scipy.sparse as sp
    except Exception:
        return None, None

    tstart = time.time()
    tmax = max(1.0, float(time_limit) - (time.time() - tstart))
    if tmax <= 1.0:
        return None, None

    half = n // 2
    m_pairs = int(n * (n - 1) // 2)
    total_vars = n + m_pairs + 1
    bvar = n + m_pairs

    data = []
    rids = []
    cids = []
    rhs = []
    row = 0

    for j in range(1, n):
        base = j * (j - 1) // 2
        for i in range(j):
            z = n + base + i

            data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(z)
            data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(i)
            rhs.append(0.0); row += 1

            data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(z)
            data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(j)
            rhs.append(0.0); row += 1

            data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(z)
            data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(i)
            data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(j)
            rhs.append(-1.0); row += 1

    for k in range(1, n):
        # P_k
        for i in range(n - k):
            j = i + k
            zidx = j * (j - 1) // 2 + i
            data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(i)
            data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(n + zidx)
        data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(bvar)
        rhs.append(0.0); row += 1

        # Q_k
        for i in range(n - k):
            j = i + k
            zidx = j * (j - 1) // 2 + i
            data.append(1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(j)
            data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(n + zidx)
        data.append(-1.0); rids.append(row); cids.append(bvar)
        rhs.append(0.0); row += 1

    try:
        A_ub = sp.csr_matrix((data, (rids, cids)), shape=(row, total_vars))
        b_ub = np.asarray(rhs, dtype=float)
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, total_vars), dtype=float)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([float(half)], dtype=float)

        c = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=float)
        c[bvar] = 1.0

        integrality = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=int)
        integrality[-1] = 0

        lb = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=float)
        ub = np.ones(total_vars, dtype=float)
        ub[-1] = float(half)
        lb[-1] = 0.0

        constraints = [
            LinearConstraint(A_ub, b_ub, np.full(row, np.inf)),
            LinearConstraint(A_eq, b_eq, b_eq)
        ]
        bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
        options = {"time_limit": int(max(1, tmax - (time.time() - tstart)))}
        res = milp(c, constraints, integrality, bounds, options=options)
    except Exception:
        return None, None

    x = None
    if hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
        st = getattr(res, "status", None)
        if st in (0, 1) or st is None:
            x = res.x
    if x is None:
        return None, None

    A = np.rint(np.asarray(x[:n], dtype=float)).astype(int)
    A = np.clip(A, 0, 1)
    if int(np.sum(A)) != half:
        return None, None

    corr = np.correlate(A, 1 - A, mode="full")
    mmax = int(np.max(corr))
    cval = float(mmax) * (2.0 / float(n))
    if cval < float(limit_c5) - 1e-12:
        return A.tolist(), mmax
    return None, None


def cont_dir_vals(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    P = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    Q = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    P[0] = float(np.dot(h, 1.0 - h))
    Q[0] = P[0]
    for k in range(1, n):
        a = h[:-k]
        b = h[k:]
        P[k] = float(np.dot(a, 1.0 - b))
        Q[k] = float(np.dot(b, 1.0 - a))
    return P, Q


def cont_maxc(h):
    P, Q = cont_dir_vals(h)
    mx = max(float(P.max()), float(Q.max()))
    return mx * (2.0 / float(len(h)))


def smooth_max_value(h, beta):
    P, Q = cont_dir_vals(h)
    n = len(h)
    vals = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    vals[:n] = P
    vals[n:] = Q[1:]
    mx = float(vals.max())
    z = np.clip(float(beta) * (vals - mx), -60.0, 60.0)
    ex = np.exp(z)
    return (mx + float(np.log(np.sum(ex))) / float(beta)) * (2.0 / float(n))


def smooth_max_grad(h, beta):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    P, Q = cont_dir_vals(h)
    vals = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    vals[:n] = P
    vals[n:] = Q[1:]
    mx = float(vals.max())
    z = np.clip(float(beta) * (vals - mx), -60.0, 60.0)
    ex = np.exp(z)
    tot = float(np.sum(ex))
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k in range(n):
        w = float(ex[k]) / tot
        if w <= 1e-12:
            continue
        if k == 0:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        else:
            g[:n - k] += w * (1.0 - h[k:])
            g[k:] -= w * h[:-k]
    for idx in range(n - 1):
        k = idx + 1
        w = float(ex[n + idx]) / tot
        if w <= 1e-12:
            continue
        g[k:] += w * (1.0 - h[:-k])
        g[:n - k] -= w * h[k:]
    return g * (2.0 / float(n))


def sm_fun(h):
    return smooth_max_value(h, _SM_BETA)


def sm_jac(h):
    return smooth_max_grad(h, _SM_BETA)


def sm_sum(h):
    return float(np.sum(h) - _SM_TARGET)


def sm_sum_jac(h):
    return np.ones(len(h), dtype=float)


def run_slsqp_smooth(h0, beta, maxiter, ftol):
    global _SM_BETA, _SM_TARGET
    n = len(h0)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    _SM_BETA = float(beta)
    _SM_TARGET = float(target)
    h0 = project_box_sum(h0, target)
    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))
    constraints = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sm_sum, "jac": sm_sum_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(sm_fun, h0, method="SLSQP", jac=sm_jac,
                       bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints,
                       options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": float(ftol)})
        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            return project_box_sum(res.x, target)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h0


def cont_grad_P(k, h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k == 0:
        return 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    g[:n - k] += 1.0 - h[k:]
    g[k:] -= h[:-k]
    return g


def cont_grad_Q(k, h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k == 0:
        return 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    g[k:] += 1.0 - h[:-k]
    g[:n - k] -= h[k:]
    return g


def lp_refine(h, time_limit, radius=0.03):
    n = len(h)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    P, Q = cont_dir_vals(h)
    maxv = max(float(P.max()), float(Q.max()))
    maxc = maxv * (2.0 / float(n))
    tstart = time.time()
    end_t = tstart + max(1e-6, float(time_limit))
    no_improve = 0

    while time.time() < end_t and no_improve < 8:
        active = []
        tol = max(1e-8, 0.03 * maxv)
        for k in range(n):
            if float(P[k]) >= maxv - tol:
                active.append((float(P[k]), 0, k))
        for k in range(1, n):
            if float(Q[k]) >= maxv - tol:
                active.append((float(Q[k]), 1, k))
        active.sort(reverse=True)
        if len(active) > 80:
            active = active[:80]
        if not active:
            break

        rows = []
        bs = []
        for val, typ, k in active:
            g = cont_grad_P(k, h) if typ == 0 else cont_grad_Q(k, h)
            row = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
            row[:n] = g
            row[n] = -1.0
            rows.append(row)
            bs.append(-float(val))

        improved = False
        if rows is not None and linprog is not None and rows:
            A = np.vstack(rows)
            b = np.asarray(bs, dtype=float)
            Aeq = np.ones(n + 1, dtype=float)
            Aeq[n] = 0.0
            beq = 0.0
            cobj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
            cobj[n] = 1.0
            bounds = []
            for hv in h:
                lo = max(-float(radius), -float(hv))
                up = min(float(radius), 1.0 - float(hv))
                bounds.append((lo, up))
            bounds.append((0.0, None))
            try:
                res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq,
                              bounds=bounds, method="highs")
                if res is not None and (getattr(res, "success", False) or getattr(res, "status", 1) == 0):
                    d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
                    best = maxc
                    best_h = h
                    for theta in (1.0, 0.6, 0.35, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
                        cand = project_box_sum(h + float(theta) * d, target)
                        Pc, Qc = cont_dir_vals(cand)
                        mc = max(float(Pc.max()), float(Qc.max())) * (2.0 / float(n))
                        if mc < best - 1e-12:
                            best = mc
                            best_h = cand
                    if best < maxc - 1e-12:
                        h = best_h
                        maxc = best
                        improved = True
            except Exception:
                pass

        if not improved:
            try:
                g = smooth_max_grad(h, 20.0)
                best = maxc
                best_h = h
                lr = 0.02
                for _ in range(5):
                    cand = project_box_sum(h - float(lr) * g, target)
                    Pc, Qc = cont_dir_vals(cand)
                    mc = max(float(Pc.max()), float(Qc.max())) * (2.0 / float(n))
                    if mc < best - 1e-12:
                        best = mc
                        best_h = cand
                    lr *= 0.5
                if best < maxc - 1e-12:
                    h = best_h
                    maxc = best
                    improved = True
            except Exception:
                pass

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 3:
                break

    h = project_box_sum(h, target)
    P, Q = cont_dir_vals(h)
    maxc = max(float(P.max()), float(Q.max())) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return h, maxc


def continuous_refine(hstart, n, budget_s, current_best):
    tstart = time.time()
    end_t = tstart + max(1e-6, float(budget_s))
    h = project_box_sum(hstart, float(n) / 2.0)
    c = compute_c5(h)

    if end_t - tstart > 25.0:
        if n <= 105:
            schedule = [(5.0, 45), (20.0, 35), (60.0, 25), (150.0, 20)]
        else:
            schedule = [(10.0, 30), (40.0, 25), (100.0, 20)]
        for beta, maxiter in schedule:
            if end_t - time.time() < 12.0:
                break
            h2 = run_slsqp_smooth(h, beta, maxiter, 1e-10)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2)
            if c2 < c - 1e-12:
                h = h2
                c = c2

    rem = end_t - time.time()
    if rem > 8.0:
        h, c = lp_refine(h, min(70.0, rem * 0.85), 0.025)

    h = project_box_sum(h, float(n) / 2.0)
    c = compute_c5(h)
    hq = quantize_exact(h, 64)
    cq = compute_c5(hq)
    if cq < c:
        h = hq
        c = cq
    return h, c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    target = TARGET_C5

    parent = None
    try:
        if len(initial_h_values) > 0:
            parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or len(parent) == 0:
        rng0 = np.random.RandomState(seed)
        best_h = np.asarray(random_binary(120, rng0), dtype=float)
        best_n = 120
    else:
        best_h = parent.copy()
        best_n = int(len(best_h))

    best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
    best_bin_seq = None
    best_from_binary = False

    if deadline - t0 <= 10.0:
        return best_h, best_c5, best_n

    n_list = [120, 100, 128, 144, 160, 112, 136, 148, 192]
    reserve_cont = 250.0 if budget_s > 800 else 80.0
    binary_end = deadline - reserve_cont
    idxn = 0

    while idxn < len(n_list) and time.time() < binary_end - 25.0:
        n = int(n_list[idxn])
        idxn += 1
        allowed = int(np.floor((target - 1e-12) * float(n) / 2.0))
        if allowed < 4:
            continue
        rem = binary_end - time.time()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        slice_s = min(240.0, max(45.0, rem * 0.30))
        if n > 150:
            slice_s = min(slice_s, 165.0)
        seq, m = binary_search_n(n, allowed, parent, int(seed) + 1000 + n, slice_s)
        if seq is None:
            continue
        if len(seq) != n or sum(seq) != n // 2:
            continue
        hf = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float)
        c = compute_c5(hf)
        if c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = hf
            best_c5 = c
            best_n = n
            best_bin_seq = seq
            best_from_binary = True
            if best_c5 < target:
                binary_end = deadline - 30.0

    if best_from_binary and best_c5 < target and time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
        try:
            hlist = [int(round(float(x))) for x in best_bin_seq]
            P, Q, m = init_binary_state(hlist)
            sfr = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) + 7771)
            time_slice = max(1.0, min(300.0, deadline - time.time() - 20.0))
            h2, m2 = binary_improve(hlist, P, Q, m, time_slice, sfr, max(1, m - 1))
            if m2 < m:
                hf = np.asarray(h2, dtype=float)
                c = compute_c5(hf)
                if c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_h = hf
                    best_c5 = c
                    best_n = len(hf)
                    best_bin_seq = h2
                    best_from_binary = True
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        for n_mip in (64, 72, 80):
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            if rem < 100.0:
                break
            time_slice = min(90.0, rem * 0.4)
            seqm, mmax = binary_mip(n_mip, min(target, best_c5), time_slice)
            if seqm is not None:
                hf = np.asarray(seqm, dtype=float)
                c = compute_c5(hf)
                if c < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_h = hf
                    best_c5 = c
                    best_n = n_mip
                    best_bin_seq = seqm
                    best_from_binary = True
                    if best_c5 <= 0.372:
                        break

    if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
        cont_budget = min(240.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.time() - 10.0))
        if best_c5 < target:
            cont_budget = min(120.0, cont_budget)
        if cont_budget > 60.0:
            cont_start = time.time()
            candidates = []
            seen = set()
            if parent is not None:
                pn = int(len(parent))
                if pn not in seen:
                    seen.add(pn)
                    candidates.append((parent, pn))
                for pref in (100, 120):
                    if pref not in seen:
                        seen.add(pref)
                        hinterp = interp_continuous_to_n(pref, parent)
                        candidates.append((hinterp, pref))
            bn = int(best_n)
            if bn not in seen:
                seen.add(bn)
                candidates.append((best_h, bn))

            for hst, nn in candidates:
                used = time.time() - cont_start
                if cont_budget - used < 35.0:
                    break
                slice_s = min(120.0, cont_budget - used)
                if slice_s < 30.0:
                    break
                try:
                    grow, crow = continuous_refine(hst, nn, slice_s, best_c5)
                    if grow is not None and crow < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                        best_h = np.asarray(grow, dtype=float)
                        best_c5 = crow
                        best_n = nn
                        best_bin_seq = None
                        best_from_binary = False
                except Exception:
                    pass

    best_h = np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float)
    if best_h.ndim != 1:
        best_h = best_h.ravel()
    best_n = int(best_h.shape[0])
    if best_n > 0 and abs(float(np.sum(best_h)) - float(best_n) / 2.0) > 1e-5:
        best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, float(best_n) / 2.0)
    final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, final_c5, best_n
```
